\+\_\+get\+Atomic\+Nf\+U\+\_\+\+T\+\_\+\+Ptr Template\+: get Atomic Natural value From Universal format 

Extracts an ZType\+\_\+\+Atomic field from a ZData\+Buffer (Universal format according ZType\+\_\+type) \+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item using the length deduced from its originary type.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Data value byte order is reversed according Endian convention IF NECESSARY (if system is little Endian).


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}

\+\_\+get\+Atomic\+UfN suite Template\+: get Atomic Universal value (URF header excepted) From Natural value according its data type (ZType\+\_\+type) given by p\+ZType 

Converts a Atomic Natural field to an Universal format value reverse byte order if necessary (Endian) and returns
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item a ZData\+Buffer with the formatted field content ready to be used as a natural data type in the given ZData\+Buffer p\+Out\+Data
\item the atomic value data field as well as a return value \begin{DoxyVerb}Size of the resulting field is the size of natural data type (ex : int8_t is 1 ), and Size field of ZDataBuffer pOutData is set to correct length.


          @param[in] pInData       Atomic value to format field from
              @param[out] pOutData     Universal value written to ZDataBuffer, eventually extended according routine
      @param[in] pZType        Type of data ( ZType_type )
\end{DoxyVerb}
 
\end{DoxyItemize}

\+\_\+get\+Atomic\+UfN Obtains a ZData\+Buffer content with a Universal format from a ZData\+Buffer with Natural atomic value 

Uses \+\_\+get\+Atomic\+Uf\+N\+\_\+T template routine to pack field according system constraints (big / little endian) and leading sign byte.


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Size of the returned ZData\+Buffer content is
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item size of Atomic data + 1 byte (leading sign byte) if signed value type,
\item size of Atomic data if unsigned value type.
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item Byte order is reversed if system is little Endian
\end{DoxyItemize}

ZIndex\+Field\+\_\+struct details reminder
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item ssize\+\_\+t Offset; \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} Offset of the Field from the beginning of record
\item ssize\+\_\+t Natural\+Size; \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} Length of the Field to extract from the record \+: natural size is the canonical data size and not the size of the data once it has been reprocessed for key usage. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Special case for char and uchar \+: Sizes are equal to Cstring size (obtained with strlen).
\end{DoxyNote}

\item ssize\+\_\+t Key\+Field\+Size; \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} length of the field when stored into Key (Field dictionary internal format size)
\item long ZType; \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} Type mask of the Field \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{zindexedfile_2ztypetype__old__tobe__deleted_8h_a9e2a9f93afe06c279c78b5606731f3e8}{ZType\+\_\+type}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\item URF\+\_\+\+Array\+\_\+\+Count\+\_\+type Array\+Size; \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} in case of ZType is ZType\+\_\+\+Array \+: number of elements in the Array(Atomic size can be computed then using Natural\+Size / Array\+Size or Key\+Size / Array\+Size ). For other storage type, this field is set to 1.
\end{DoxyItemize}


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+In\+Data} & ZData\+Buffer containing the user record to extract field from. \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em p\+Out\+Data} & ZData\+Buffer containing as a return the packed/extracted field value converted as ready to be used as a key field \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+Field} & ZIndex Key dictionary rank (ZIndex\+Field\+\_\+struct) describing the data field to extract. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

a ZStatus. In case of error, ZStatus is returned and ZException is set with appropriate message.\+see\+: \doxylink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{ZBSError} 
\end{DoxyReturn}

ZKey\+::set\+Field\+Value loads the value (auto) p\+Value to the key field (defined in key dictionary) of rank p\+Field\+Rank. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
GNU C++ compiler converts arrays to pointers when using \textquotesingle{}auto\textquotesingle{} clause. In case of arrays, use template ZKey\+::set\+Field\+ValueT 

p\+Array\+Size is not necessary in case of data type that is anything excepted a pointer. In case of pointer of chars \textquotesingle{}char\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\textquotesingle{} (C string), p\+Arraysize is not necessary because deduced from size of string. In case of pointer to std\+::string, p\+Arraysize is not necessary because deduced from size of string.
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Bug}
\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{bug__bug000001}{Bug}}]the default value of -\/1 for p\+Array\+Size does not work with GNU compiler because of the use of \textquotesingle{}auto\textquotesingle{} clause. so that, p\+Array\+Size should systematically be mentionned\end{DoxyRefDesc}


This routine controls the coherence of given data.
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item It makes the data conversion whenever required to have a dictionary defined data (natural data).
\item It makes the conversion from natural field to internal key field value format.
\item It addresses pointers vs arrays.
\item It pads the key if the size of input data (specifically for arrays) is not large enough.
\item It truncates the data if given value exceeds defined key field boundaries.
\end{DoxyItemize}


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+Field\+Rank} & field order (rank) in key dictionary \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+Value} & Value of undertermined type to set the field with \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+Array\+Size} & Usefull value for pointers only, and for It gives the corresponding number of atomic values the pointer points to. If data type is other than pointer, array size value is deduced from data type. A negative value means \textquotesingle{}omitted\textquotesingle{}. Defaulted to -\/1. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
a ZStatus. In case of error, ZStatus is returned and ZException is set with appropriate message.
\end{DoxyReturn}
